# GitHub Foundations: human review packet

225 original practice questions. No mock exam or Copilot installation is required to review this file.

## How to review

1. Read the English question, all four choices and explanations, the concept, hints, and linked official documentation.
2. Check English content approved only after personally verifying the English content. You do not need to review another language.
3. A separate reviewer can expand Spanish or Brazilian Portuguese and check only that translation, verifying meaning, option order, explanations, hints, and documentation references against the approved English content.
4. Leave uncertain or incorrect questions unchecked. Send the edited Markdown file back to the maintainer, with suggested corrections separately.

English content and each translation have separate approvals. Translation approval requires current English approval, imported previously or checked in this same packet.
Changing English content or its supporting source invalidates all three approvals. Changing one translation invalidates only that language's approval.
The importer rejects stale revisions or changed question content. Do not edit prompts, answers, or explanations in this review copy.
Unchecked boxes do not revoke existing approvals. These are honor-system human attestations, not proof of reviewer identity.
AI must never check these boxes or attest to human review. These are original unofficial practice items, not real exam questions.
This packet intentionally includes the full answer key. It contains no learner answers, scores, chats, or progress.

Search for `- [ ] English content approved`, `- [ ] Spanish translation approved`, or `- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved` to find unchecked items.
Technical names and official documentation links may remain in English. Translation review verifies that references support the same meaning; it does not require translating the linked external documentation.

## Question 1: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-001`

**Content revision:** `42c51c0b13fc680ef675baa6e10773afab8d8e8b43d2408ec2d14a4e7ddb1521`

**en review revision:** `66d5ba031b8a45db4a5925ee5d63a910e7918a34286c73f7d4b31f103eda3c8f`
**es review revision:** `8040276078f4239ffad8fc13edec9ab83335b4825ad005f61704b61118d40cbc`
**pt-BR review revision:** `8016ff7fd5378549d0cca42e57ecd757d84eea29e0e39f89088ac09bc76edc81`

### English

Which information does version control preserve about a project?

1. Changes and their history **[CORRECT]**

   Version control records changes over time.

2. Only the latest file contents

   Keeping only the latest copy loses earlier revisions.

3. Only the current branch name

   Branch names alone do not describe changes.

4. Files and their execution output

   Execution output is not recorded automatically.

**Concept:** Version control makes earlier states and the evolution of a project inspectable.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué información conserva el control de versiones sobre un proyecto?

1. Los cambios y su historial **[CORRECT]**

   El control de versiones registra cambios a lo largo del tiempo.

2. Solo el contenido más reciente

   Guardar solo la copia más reciente pierde revisiones anteriores.

3. Solo el nombre de la rama actual

   Los nombres de ramas no describen por sí solos los cambios.

4. Los archivos y su salida de ejecución

   La salida de ejecución no se registra automáticamente.

**Concept:** El control de versiones permite inspeccionar estados anteriores y la evolución del proyecto.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Quais informações o controle de versão preserva sobre um projeto?

1. As alterações e seu histórico **[CORRECT]**

   O controle de versão registra alterações ao longo do tempo.

2. Apenas o conteúdo mais recente

   Guardar apenas a cópia mais recente perde revisões anteriores.

3. Apenas o nome da branch atual

   Nomes de branches não descrevem as alterações por si só.

4. Os arquivos e sua saída de execução

   A saída de execução não é registrada automaticamente.

**Concept:** O controle de versão permite inspecionar estados anteriores e a evolução do projeto.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

## Question 2: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-002`

**Content revision:** `5f5dee9eef84f43b7860b4a0d47ca1e92317d005c1dc194e3f6b85d7e8372da5`

**en review revision:** `272f894125f5777a4a6e05a987c82cb1edbe878ae267d0dc49586080827e1e91`
**es review revision:** `4de1703c7cafd3d9039a8a26eacea026d463039f3617dd84ae9bf70944a21c66`
**pt-BR review revision:** `867840fd13fd198e2945286efd9bf706879ffdcc227452b9653bb9d5c636fd08`

### English

Which pairing correctly identifies Git and GitHub?

1. Package registry; code editor

   Git is not a package registry.

2. Version control system; collaboration platform **[CORRECT]**

   Git versions files; GitHub hosts Git-based collaboration.

3. Collaboration platform; version control system

   This reverses the roles of the two products.

4. Code editor; package registry

   Git is not a code editor.

**Concept:** Git can work independently; GitHub adds hosted collaboration around repositories.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Match the interface to where the task is performed.
- Separate Git operations from GitHub-hosted collaboration features.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué pareja identifica correctamente Git y GitHub?

1. Registro de paquetes; editor de código

   Git no es un registro de paquetes.

2. Sistema de control de versiones; plataforma colaborativa **[CORRECT]**

   Git versiona archivos; GitHub aloja colaboración basada en Git.

3. Plataforma colaborativa; sistema de control de versiones

   Esta opción invierte las funciones de ambos productos.

4. Editor de código; registro de paquetes

   Git no es un editor de código.

**Concept:** Git funciona de forma independiente; GitHub añade colaboración alojada sobre repositorios.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Relaciona la interfaz con el lugar donde se realiza la tarea.
- Distingue las operaciones de Git de las funciones colaborativas de GitHub.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual combinação identifica corretamente Git e GitHub?

1. Registro de pacotes; editor de código

   Git não é um registro de pacotes.

2. Sistema de controle de versão; plataforma colaborativa **[CORRECT]**

   Git versiona arquivos; GitHub hospeda colaboração baseada em Git.

3. Plataforma colaborativa; sistema de controle de versão

   Esta opção inverte as funções dos dois produtos.

4. Editor de código; registro de pacotes

   Git não é um editor de código.

**Concept:** Git funciona independentemente; GitHub adiciona colaboração hospedada aos repositórios.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Relacione a interface ao local onde a tarefa é realizada.
- Diferencie as operações do Git dos recursos colaborativos do GitHub.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

## Question 3: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-003`

**Content revision:** `803cc0c32d23c7c0dd457e2d52196b1cef16433e738395bc242c3115c6210f63`

**en review revision:** `480a3215221b05582e21a0862704ac5adf9ed7f2579de5d9f99cefc668ea0f7f`
**es review revision:** `95fc728d42d8769cab09fc15cf63b6b5e1bd436ceedb249c23e22b37f988e0bb`
**pt-BR review revision:** `9f6812c36d74ad8a71cdcad2e838664af1bce344f06824b4fa64eebb5ff68d0f`

### English

Which command creates a local Git repository from an existing remote?

1. git fetch https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Fetch retrieves objects into an existing local repository.

2. git pull https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Pull operates within an existing local repository.

3. git clone https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git **[CORRECT]**

   Clone creates a repository with remote history locally.

4. git init https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Init initializes a repository rather than copying remote history.

**Concept:** Cloning creates the local repository and configures its initial remote.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué comando crea un repositorio Git local a partir de uno remoto existente?

1. git fetch https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Fetch obtiene objetos en un repositorio local existente.

2. git pull https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Pull opera dentro de un repositorio local existente.

3. git clone https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git **[CORRECT]**

   Clone crea un repositorio local con el historial remoto.

4. git init https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Init inicializa un repositorio, no copia el historial remoto.

**Concept:** Clonar crea el repositorio local y configura su remoto inicial.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual comando cria um repositório Git local a partir de um remoto existente?

1. git fetch https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Fetch obtém objetos em um repositório local existente.

2. git pull https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Pull opera dentro de um repositório local existente.

3. git clone https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git **[CORRECT]**

   Clone cria um repositório local com o histórico remoto.

4. git init https://github.com/octo-org/handbook.git

   Init inicializa um repositório, sem copiar o histórico remoto.

**Concept:** Clonar cria o repositório local e configura seu remoto inicial.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie o histórico local do histórico de outro repositório.
- Verifique se é necessário baixar, integrar ou enviar alterações.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/getting-changes-from-a-remote-repository>

**Question ID:** `ghf-004`

**Content revision:** `e2d8b9f69c37ab70d591196f5467582f201c84020b439308cfb7d6c997b99455`

**en review revision:** `eca00cd7aa8134f2963f8c4599f3bca923d11315d9985548bf9b9ceadc46a043`
**es review revision:** `a707fc78611c9ec8fee14462a4e6ad26d6ee139c8fd225dc71fbb74399ff5ef4`
**pt-BR review revision:** `f93c48188bceef202d176875787bed60d08e2bd7bd8e434354de1c94af3c6ca9`

### English

Which command stages only the current changes in README.md?

1. git commit README.md

   Commit records a snapshot instead of only staging content.

2. git push README.md

   Push sends commits to a remote repository.

3. git diff README.md

   Diff displays changes without staging them.

4. git add README.md **[CORRECT]**

   Add places the file's current content in the staging area.

**Concept:** Staging selects content for the next commit.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué comando prepara únicamente los cambios actuales de README.md?

1. git commit README.md

   Commit registra una instantánea en lugar de solo preparar contenido.

2. git push README.md

   Push envía commits a un repositorio remoto.

3. git diff README.md

   Diff muestra cambios sin prepararlos.

4. git add README.md **[CORRECT]**

   Add coloca el contenido actual del archivo en el área de preparación.

**Concept:** Preparar cambios selecciona contenido para el próximo commit.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual comando coloca somente as alterações atuais de README.md na área de staging?

1. git commit README.md

   Commit registra um snapshot em vez de apenas preparar conteúdo.

2. git push README.md

   Push envia commits para um repositório remoto.

3. git diff README.md

   Diff exibe alterações sem colocá-las no staging.

4. git add README.md **[CORRECT]**

   Add coloca o conteúdo atual do arquivo na área de staging.

**Concept:** O staging seleciona conteúdo para o próximo commit.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

**Question ID:** `ghf-005`

**Content revision:** `ef21b2d70b239322dba1f4fa2695b876ceea5c6c11014d45b0fb95cf3280adb8`

**en review revision:** `94c1a6b0ba520696bcfe3d4b612e13214dfd6ceed3ad0e6401cda66597475e60`
**es review revision:** `686aa56e27133857bd923d17973c3c576ba89f69845c67082d905bc4a5939cc2`
**pt-BR review revision:** `585ce486788419657d390c8465ae43f9e45edf78cebf31c71af0601dba0f5651`

### English

What does a Git commit record?

1. A project snapshot and metadata **[CORRECT]**

   A commit records a snapshot, message, authorship, and related metadata.

2. A live copy of all future edits

   Later edits require another commit.

3. A deployment and its runtime logs

   A commit does not itself deploy or collect logs.

4. A remote URL and its credentials

   Remote configuration is separate from project snapshots.

**Concept:** Commits represent saved project states, not ongoing working-tree edits.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué registra un commit de Git?

1. Una instantánea del proyecto y metadatos **[CORRECT]**

   Un commit registra una instantánea, mensaje, autoría y metadatos relacionados.

2. Una copia activa de toda edición futura

   Las ediciones posteriores necesitan otro commit.

3. Un despliegue y sus registros de ejecución

   Un commit no despliega ni recopila registros por sí mismo.

4. Una URL remota y sus credenciales

   La configuración remota es independiente de las instantáneas.

**Concept:** Los commits representan estados guardados, no las ediciones continuas del directorio de trabajo.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

O que um commit do Git registra?

1. Um snapshot do projeto e metadados **[CORRECT]**

   Um commit registra um snapshot, mensagem, autoria e metadados relacionados.

2. Uma cópia ativa de toda edição futura

   Edições posteriores exigem outro commit.

3. Uma implantação e seus logs de execução

   Um commit não implanta nem coleta logs por si só.

4. Uma URL remota e suas credenciais

   A configuração do remoto é separada dos snapshots.

**Concept:** Commits representam estados salvos, não as edições contínuas da árvore de trabalho.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

## Question 6: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-006`

**Content revision:** `5e4d09c19d735c5495524818102818f4b2b6df5f23df8b1ecd25997f82edf360`

**en review revision:** `6a05e7eb814445eab3d50cd8345a0aa6cbe367b61dc0ba8e23b7e427a9d125fa`
**es review revision:** `de16c7ab94506b1cc02038f1f8dd840cff15ca28ffc8570d93339400601442bf`
**pt-BR review revision:** `2cedc0e634db96b14dfb33cd12fc3c014972e7a5cbe540df479cc42a1bc9e6fc`

### English

In GitHub flow, why create a branch before editing?

1. To replace the remote repository

   A branch remains within its repository's history.

2. To isolate proposed changes **[CORRECT]**

   A branch separates ongoing work from the default branch.

3. To grant repository permissions

   Creating a branch does not grant access.

4. To publish a release immediately

   Creating a branch does not create a release.

**Concept:** Branches support isolated changes that can be reviewed before integration.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Think about the stages of a branch-based contribution.
- Separate proposing a change from making it part of another branch.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

En GitHub flow, ¿por qué se crea una rama antes de editar?

1. Para sustituir el repositorio remoto

   Una rama forma parte del historial de su repositorio.

2. Para aislar los cambios propuestos **[CORRECT]**

   Una rama separa el trabajo en curso de la rama predeterminada.

3. Para conceder permisos al repositorio

   Crear una rama no concede acceso.

4. Para publicar una versión de inmediato

   Crear una rama no crea una versión publicada.

**Concept:** Las ramas permiten aislar cambios para revisarlos antes de integrarlos.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Piensa en las etapas de una contribución basada en ramas.
- Distingue proponer un cambio de incorporarlo a otra rama.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

No GitHub flow, por que criar uma branch antes de editar?

1. Para substituir o repositório remoto

   Uma branch faz parte do histórico do seu repositório.

2. Para isolar as alterações propostas **[CORRECT]**

   Uma branch separa o trabalho em andamento da branch padrão.

3. Para conceder permissões no repositório

   Criar uma branch não concede acesso.

4. Para publicar uma versão imediatamente

   Criar uma branch não publica uma versão.

**Concept:** Branches permitem isolar alterações para revisão antes da integração.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Pense nas etapas de uma contribuição baseada em branches.
- Diferencie propor uma alteração de incorporá-la a outra branch.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-github/github-flow>

### English

Which Git operation sends local branch commits to a remote?

1. git merge

   Merge integrates histories locally.

2. git add

   Add stages local file content.

3. git push **[CORRECT]**

   Push updates the remote with local commits.

4. git fetch

   Fetch downloads remote objects and references.

**Concept:** A local commit is shared with a remote through a push.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué operación de Git envía commits de una rama local a un remoto?

1. git merge

   Merge integra historiales localmente.

2. git add

   Add prepara contenido de archivos locales.

3. git push **[CORRECT]**

   Push actualiza el remoto con commits locales.

4. git fetch

   Fetch descarga objetos y referencias remotos.

**Concept:** Un commit local se comparte con un remoto mediante push.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual operação do Git envia commits de uma branch local para um remoto?

1. git merge

   Merge integra históricos localmente.

2. git add

   Add prepara o conteúdo de arquivos locais.

3. git push **[CORRECT]**

   Push atualiza o remoto com commits locais.

4. git fetch

   Fetch baixa objetos e referências remotas.

**Concept:** Um commit local é compartilhado com um remoto por meio de push.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie o histórico local do histórico de outro repositório.
- Verifique se é necessário baixar, integrar ou enviar alterações.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

## Question 8: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-008`

**Content revision:** `7bda29e5508bb282cd93b569338bf590c63892c74b7af2fe926ab27d88a0f79f`

**en review revision:** `eec89c4deb9748add0ef8ab324689bd90e4e567c2887d3841985bf8544dbd967`
**es review revision:** `2a3c1c72ce3c6e725d61dde16bee62ca5b4cced904a90fc6c558e889718c6972`
**pt-BR review revision:** `002ddaf5415c78fc1569c13d50516da96744b68f116f642aeb0f03c481ac4be5`

### English

Which command updates remote-tracking references without integrating them into the current branch?

1. git pull origin main

   Pull also attempts to integrate the fetched changes.

2. git merge origin/main

   Merge integrates an already available history.

3. git push origin main

   Push sends local commits rather than retrieving updates.

4. git fetch origin **[CORRECT]**

   Fetch retrieves remote updates without merging them.

**Concept:** Fetching and integrating are separate steps.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué comando actualiza referencias de seguimiento remoto sin integrarlas en la rama actual?

1. git pull origin main

   Pull también intenta integrar los cambios obtenidos.

2. git merge origin/main

   Merge integra un historial ya disponible.

3. git push origin main

   Push envía commits locales, no obtiene actualizaciones.

4. git fetch origin **[CORRECT]**

   Fetch obtiene actualizaciones remotas sin fusionarlas.

**Concept:** Obtener cambios e integrarlos son pasos distintos.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual comando atualiza referências de acompanhamento remoto sem integrá-las à branch atual?

1. git pull origin main

   Pull também tenta integrar as alterações obtidas.

2. git merge origin/main

   Merge integra um histórico já disponível.

3. git push origin main

   Push envia commits locais, em vez de obter atualizações.

4. git fetch origin **[CORRECT]**

   Fetch obtém atualizações remotas sem mesclá-las.

**Concept:** Obter alterações e integrá-las são etapas distintas.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie o histórico local do histórico de outro repositório.
- Verifique se é necessário baixar, integrar ou enviar alterações.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/getting-changes-from-a-remote-repository>

## Question 9: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-009`

**Content revision:** `28865efb8cce5be0a3ad8cd4ce75ac35738b7525b1eda3f54f0eeadf6ae1d603`

**en review revision:** `4a4cac398002089d0f8cee5ad96c9c44bb940631d5bf7e9f09998e78c8d99938`
**es review revision:** `9f1dfc5812003220c6c9ea77a53053f6f6f94a45c713433ee4f1fd0567855d59`
**pt-BR review revision:** `7c41fcc1d73b847bbbdf4d2ec88bea393b9a6347c622cd72a7d9de5950b1d360`

### English

With merge-based pull configured, what does git pull do?

1. Fetches changes, then merges them **[CORRECT]**

   Merge-based pull combines fetching with merging.

2. Stages changes, then commits them

   Pull does not stage and commit working-tree edits.

3. Commits changes, then pushes them

   Pull retrieves updates rather than publishing commits.

4. Creates a branch, then checks it out

   Branch creation is not the defining pull operation.

**Concept:** Pull integrates fetched work; its integration strategy can be configured.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

Con pull configurado para fusionar, ¿qué hace git pull?

1. Obtiene cambios y después los fusiona **[CORRECT]**

   Pull basado en merge combina obtener y fusionar cambios.

2. Prepara cambios y después los confirma

   Pull no prepara ni confirma ediciones del directorio de trabajo.

3. Confirma cambios y después los sube

   Pull obtiene actualizaciones en lugar de publicar commits.

4. Crea una rama y después cambia a ella

   Crear ramas no es la operación que define pull.

**Concept:** Pull integra trabajo obtenido; su estrategia de integración se puede configurar.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Com pull configurado para merge, o que git pull faz?

1. Obtém alterações e depois as mescla **[CORRECT]**

   Pull baseado em merge combina obtenção e mesclagem.

2. Prepara alterações e depois as confirma

   Pull não prepara nem confirma edições da árvore de trabalho.

3. Confirma alterações e depois as envia

   Pull obtém atualizações em vez de publicar commits.

4. Cria uma branch e depois muda para ela

   Criar branches não é a operação que define pull.

**Concept:** Pull integra o trabalho obtido; sua estratégia de integração é configurável.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie o histórico local do histórico de outro repositório.
- Verifique se é necessário baixar, integrar ou enviar alterações.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/getting-changes-from-a-remote-repository>

## Question 10: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-010`

**Content revision:** `936658bbec35cd49b5bd30f7e8a70354fb9fb0fc2b26ab93f9555c2845f4b276`

**en review revision:** `18651d11b93ad2d351fbbc143dfdf331a16d0201ead99771eff72571f7d02b01`
**es review revision:** `0bbb3c3eb792fd48c83ee2ca426bac39bed7fa4c8eb72566569ba36437ad3f3e`
**pt-BR review revision:** `c403af4e28b3a642d14ea38cd8f8b8aaa0a4160ed0e1418192df862959ca28a0`

### English

Git cannot automatically combine competing edits to the same lines. What is this called?

1. A fast-forward merge

   A fast-forward advances a reference without divergent histories.

2. A merge conflict **[CORRECT]**

   Conflicting edits require a person to resolve the intended content.

3. A detached HEAD

   Detached HEAD describes checkout position, not competing edits.

4. A shallow clone

   A shallow clone limits downloaded history.

**Concept:** Merge conflicts require resolving content before completing integration.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Think about the stages of a branch-based contribution.
- Separate proposing a change from making it part of another branch.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

Git no puede combinar automáticamente ediciones incompatibles de las mismas líneas. ¿Cómo se llama esto?

1. Una fusión de avance rápido

   Un avance rápido mueve una referencia sin historiales divergentes.

2. Un conflicto de fusión **[CORRECT]**

   Los cambios incompatibles requieren resolver el contenido deseado.

3. Un HEAD separado

   HEAD separado describe la posición de checkout, no ediciones incompatibles.

4. Un clon superficial

   Un clon superficial limita el historial descargado.

**Concept:** Los conflictos de fusión requieren resolver el contenido antes de completar la integración.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Piensa en las etapas de una contribución basada en ramas.
- Distingue proponer un cambio de incorporarlo a otra rama.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

O Git não consegue combinar automaticamente edições incompatíveis nas mesmas linhas. Como isso se chama?

1. Um merge fast-forward

   Fast-forward avança uma referência sem históricos divergentes.

2. Um conflito de merge **[CORRECT]**

   Alterações incompatíveis exigem resolver o conteúdo desejado.

3. Um HEAD destacado

   HEAD destacado descreve a posição de checkout, não edições incompatíveis.

4. Um clone superficial

   Um clone superficial limita o histórico baixado.

**Concept:** Conflitos de merge exigem resolver o conteúdo antes de concluir a integração.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Pense nas etapas de uma contribuição baseada em branches.
- Diferencie propor uma alteração de incorporá-la a outra branch.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-github/github-flow>

## Question 11: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-011`

**Content revision:** `873bb69d9c370f3e70c53d14ad37d2a2fd3a01c56c536ef9ef0ccf3b4c800205`

**en review revision:** `869c9638d3d345622ff6376278096ecb96eb19deed94332e6ec4a5ba68b5a00f`
**es review revision:** `dd1ed9718db4a27e92c60fbf3536db0f22a4a0ed7d6076bd2e50ec54ce378b7d`
**pt-BR review revision:** `5cc3384ca2038ed247ab7f66fb46d065d4be8c333895d05c4395a37009e64428`

### English

Which operation undoes a published commit by adding a new inverse commit?

1. Rebase

   Rebase reapplies commits onto a different base.

2. Amend

   Amend replaces the most recent commit.

3. Revert **[CORRECT]**

   Revert records a new commit that reverses an earlier change.

4. Reset

   Reset moves a reference rather than adding an inverse commit.

**Concept:** Reverting preserves the existing shared history and adds an undo change.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Think about the stages of a branch-based contribution.
- Separate proposing a change from making it part of another branch.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué operación deshace un commit publicado añadiendo un nuevo commit inverso?

1. Rebase

   Rebase vuelve a aplicar commits sobre otra base.

2. Amend

   Amend sustituye el commit más reciente.

3. Revert **[CORRECT]**

   Revert registra un commit que invierte un cambio anterior.

4. Reset

   Reset mueve una referencia en lugar de añadir un commit inverso.

**Concept:** Revertir conserva el historial compartido existente y añade un cambio que deshace otro.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Piensa en las etapas de una contribución basada en ramas.
- Distingue proponer un cambio de incorporarlo a otra rama.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual operação desfaz um commit publicado adicionando um novo commit inverso?

1. Rebase

   Rebase reaplica commits sobre outra base.

2. Amend

   Amend substitui o commit mais recente.

3. Revert **[CORRECT]**

   Revert registra um commit que inverte uma alteração anterior.

4. Reset

   Reset move uma referência em vez de adicionar um commit inverso.

**Concept:** Reverter preserva o histórico compartilhado existente e adiciona uma alteração inversa.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Pense nas etapas de uma contribuição baseada em branches.
- Diferencie propor uma alteração de incorporá-la a outra branch.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/pull-requests/how-tos/merge-and-close-pull-requests/reverting-a-pull-request>

## Question 12: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-012`

**Content revision:** `17296c5521263775e297e355e2731457516297aab5cde0e681a1b0063129fd6e`

**en review revision:** `70e556a73b9335d7482aed3c7ac964691cbaaf4a13da590aa03e9ba044f7f6b6`
**es review revision:** `756db8872bc212e6660bfeff9d3abeb0f70a4ee1bbc7b787565b21ae13588baf`
**pt-BR review revision:** `d79ec5bea7deabe6bf67cd416731c81ea103d0eba6a99ac3fe711aaf255c671b`

### English

Which command records the already staged changes with a message?

1. git add -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Add stages content; this is not its message syntax.

2. git push -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Push transfers commits; it does not create this commit.

3. git status -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Status reports state; it does not create commits.

4. git commit -m "Correct setup instructions" **[CORRECT]**

   Commit records staged content and the supplied message.

**Concept:** Staging chooses content; committing records that content with a message.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué comando registra los cambios ya preparados con un mensaje?

1. git add -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Add prepara contenido; esta no es su sintaxis de mensajes.

2. git push -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Push transfiere commits, no crea este commit.

3. git status -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Status informa del estado, no crea commits.

4. git commit -m "Correct setup instructions" **[CORRECT]**

   Commit registra el contenido preparado y el mensaje indicado.

**Concept:** Preparar selecciona contenido; confirmar lo registra con un mensaje.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual comando registra as alterações já preparadas com uma mensagem?

1. git add -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Add prepara conteúdo; esta não é sua sintaxe de mensagens.

2. git push -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Push transfere commits, não cria este commit.

3. git status -m "Correct setup instructions"

   Status informa o estado, não cria commits.

4. git commit -m "Correct setup instructions" **[CORRECT]**

   Commit registra o conteúdo preparado e a mensagem indicada.

**Concept:** O staging seleciona conteúdo; o commit o registra com uma mensagem.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

**Content revision:** `cd977724e34d382d8c8d55919e920909f1641f5cf273fb3368573ab3435e2610`

### English

Which command lists configured Git remotes and their fetch and push URLs?

1. git remote -v **[CORRECT]**

   Remote with -v displays remote names and URLs.

2. git branch -v

   Branch lists branches and related commit information.

3. git status -s

   Status summarizes working-tree and index changes.

4. git log --oneline

   Log displays recorded commit history.

**Concept:** Remote configuration associates names with repository connection URLs.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué comando muestra los remotos de Git configurados y sus URL de fetch y push?

1. git remote -v **[CORRECT]**

   Remote con -v muestra los nombres y URL de los remotos.

2. git branch -v

   Branch enumera ramas e información de commits.

3. git status -s

   Status resume cambios del directorio de trabajo y del índice.

4. git log --oneline

   Log muestra el historial de commits registrado.

**Concept:** La configuración de remotos asocia nombres con URL de conexión a repositorios.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

1. git remote -v **[CORRECT]**

   Remote com -v mostra os nomes e URLs dos remotos.

2. git branch -v

   Branch lista branches e informações de commits.

3. git status -s

   Status resume alterações da árvore de trabalho e do índice.

4. git log --oneline

   Log mostra o histórico de commits registrado.

**Concept:** A configuração de remotos associa nomes a URLs de conexão com repositórios.

**Authored coaching hints:**

## Question 14: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-014`

**Content revision:** `45d0570ea717107f75ad7445d53f9ad39cfc7397458c2a751e1238799dc1208d`

**en review revision:** `3efbeb2329172c0f7546e955d694fa957163ec20a13c308070fac948c0777d90`
**es review revision:** `ad4769fbf142c7ade7e671d86b618c170f1ebca118074f086e5802dd9ed95538`
**pt-BR review revision:** `b1a4a70b4bbf934a465757c5fa0b462e03445144ce8b6c2f8b337eb02968c650`

### English

Which sequence follows the core GitHub flow?

1. Commit -&gt; merge -&gt; branch -&gt; pull request

   A feature branch is created before its changes are proposed.

2. Branch -&gt; commit -&gt; pull request -&gt; merge **[CORRECT]**

   Work is isolated, recorded, proposed, and then integrated.

3. Pull request -&gt; merge -&gt; branch -&gt; commit

   A proposal needs a branch containing changes before merging.

4. Merge -&gt; commit -&gt; pull request -&gt; branch

   Merging precedes neither the work nor its proposal.

**Concept:** GitHub flow organizes branch-based work around pull request review.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Think about the stages of a branch-based contribution.
- Separate proposing a change from making it part of another branch.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué secuencia sigue el flujo principal de GitHub flow?

1. Commit -&gt; fusión -&gt; rama -&gt; pull request

   La rama de trabajo se crea antes de proponer sus cambios.

2. Rama -&gt; commit -&gt; pull request -&gt; fusión **[CORRECT]**

   El trabajo se aísla, registra, propone y después se integra.

3. Pull request -&gt; fusión -&gt; rama -&gt; commit

   Una propuesta necesita una rama con cambios antes de fusionarse.

4. Fusión -&gt; commit -&gt; pull request -&gt; rama

   La fusión no precede ni al trabajo ni a su propuesta.

**Concept:** GitHub flow organiza el trabajo en ramas mediante revisión de pull requests.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Piensa en las etapas de una contribución basada en ramas.
- Distingue proponer un cambio de incorporarlo a otra rama.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual sequência segue o fluxo principal do GitHub flow?

1. Commit -&gt; merge -&gt; branch -&gt; pull request

   A branch de trabalho é criada antes de propor suas alterações.

2. Branch -&gt; commit -&gt; pull request -&gt; merge **[CORRECT]**

   O trabalho é isolado, registrado, proposto e depois integrado.

3. Pull request -&gt; merge -&gt; branch -&gt; commit

   Uma proposta precisa de uma branch com alterações antes do merge.

4. Merge -&gt; commit -&gt; pull request -&gt; branch

   O merge não precede o trabalho nem sua proposta.

**Concept:** GitHub flow organiza o trabalho em branches por meio da revisão de pull requests.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Pense nas etapas de uma contribuição baseada em branches.
- Diferencie propor uma alteração de incorporá-la a outra branch.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-github/github-flow>

## Question 15: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-015`

**Content revision:** `407bb97ce2f2852a143915c4177453736620217a23f058f598367a3faf27d787`

**en review revision:** `04e49fa96b17bb7eee16906cce1f528d3924d2c8cb335e83c25103c0bd81543e`
**es review revision:** `23b0810d46154227ce08974e2c51da6341c16ab6d326228221f5338a4baa9d72`
**pt-BR review revision:** `4cf713ea14d6195a69418b1049c056525f10f46b59763251be255e0104a54b6a`

### English

What happens when another commit is pushed to an open pull request's head branch?

1. The pull request merges automatically

   New commits do not themselves authorize merging.

2. The base branch moves to that commit

   The base branch changes only through a separate update or merge.

3. The pull request includes that commit **[CORRECT]**

   The proposal tracks updates to its head branch.

4. A second pull request opens automatically

   Pushing does not automatically open another proposal.

**Concept:** An open pull request follows the current content of its head branch.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Think about the stages of a branch-based contribution.
- Separate proposing a change from making it part of another branch.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué ocurre al subir otro commit a la rama de origen de un pull request abierto?

1. El pull request se fusiona automáticamente

   Los commits nuevos no autorizan por sí mismos la fusión.

2. La rama base avanza hasta ese commit

   La rama base cambia mediante una actualización o fusión independiente.

3. El pull request incluye ese commit **[CORRECT]**

   La propuesta sigue las actualizaciones de su rama de origen.

4. Se abre otro pull request automáticamente

   Subir cambios no abre automáticamente otra propuesta.

**Concept:** Un pull request abierto refleja el contenido actual de su rama de origen.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Piensa en las etapas de una contribución basada en ramas.
- Distingue proponer un cambio de incorporarlo a otra rama.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

O que acontece ao enviar outro commit à head branch de um pull request aberto?

1. O pull request é mesclado automaticamente

   Novos commits não autorizam o merge por si só.

2. A branch base avança até esse commit

   A branch base muda por uma atualização ou merge separado.

3. O pull request inclui esse commit **[CORRECT]**

   A proposta acompanha atualizações da sua head branch.

4. Outro pull request é aberto automaticamente

   Enviar alterações não abre automaticamente outra proposta.

**Concept:** Um pull request aberto reflete o conteúdo atual da sua head branch.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Pense nas etapas de uma contribuição baseada em branches.
- Diferencie propor uma alteração de incorporá-la a outra branch.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-github/github-flow>

### English

On GitHub.com, with character shortcuts enabled and no text field focused, which sequence opens notifications?

1. G then I

   G followed by I opens repository Issues.

2. G then P

   G followed by P opens repository Pull requests.

3. G then C

   G followed by C opens repository Code.

4. G then N **[CORRECT]**

   G followed by N navigates to notifications.

**Concept:** GitHub supports page navigation using documented character-key sequences.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Check the page context and whether focus is inside a text field.
- Distinguish navigation shortcuts from text-formatting shortcuts.

3. G then C

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-017`

**Content revision:** `b4e8b8d45d13bd76ba0066bbcbc93a77d632b43abc9b7d8bc8980ad08a056983`

**en review revision:** `58d29132db1e4bed685b6b56280911a4de141f99472e2b06b011c5a2b7297c46`
**es review revision:** `47bf28d086466f994662e16adc8ba4992e822a39775e2513b65fe6cc410c3ccc`
**pt-BR review revision:** `9a7533393e978449df0e9fb76b1731d4f0d996e6c6c476af03b6761640b1e539`

### English

After a normal git clone, what does the remote name origin refer to?

1. The repository URL used for cloning **[CORRECT]**

   Clone normally configures origin for the source repository.

2. The current local branch's first commit

   Origin is a remote alias, not a commit identifier.

3. The default branch's protected status

   Protection settings do not define a remote alias.

4. The account that authored the latest commit

   Commit authorship is separate from remote configuration.

**Concept:** Origin is a conventional remote name, not a special branch or user.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

Después de un git clone normal, ¿a qué hace referencia el remoto origin?

1. A la URL del repositorio clonado **[CORRECT]**

   Clone normalmente configura origin para el repositorio de origen.

2. Al primer commit de la rama local actual

   Origin es un alias de remoto, no un identificador de commit.

3. Al estado protegido de la rama predeterminada

   La protección no define un alias de remoto.

4. A la cuenta autora del último commit

   La autoría de commits es independiente de los remotos.

**Concept:** Origin es un nombre convencional de remoto, no una rama ni un usuario especial.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Depois de um git clone normal, a que o remoto origin se refere?

1. À URL do repositório usado no clone **[CORRECT]**

   Clone normalmente configura origin para o repositório de origem.

2. Ao primeiro commit da branch local atual

   Origin é um alias de remoto, não um identificador de commit.

3. Ao estado protegido da branch padrão

   A proteção não define um alias de remoto.

4. À conta autora do último commit

   A autoria de commits é separada da configuração de remotos.

**Concept:** Origin é um nome convencional de remoto, não uma branch nem um usuário especial.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie o histórico local do histórico de outro repositório.
- Verifique se é necessário baixar, integrar ou enviar alterações.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/git-basics/managing-remote-repositories>

## Question 18: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-018`

**Content revision:** `cfe3ba7061353568d8ef7474727c1aa65c468418db2fbf0cbfde1e91ec7ce123`

**en review revision:** `e148575f1e79def6a669daf8fa05789f983072aa5afae7b9daad16f5c0c484a7`
**es review revision:** `8ba62c3d27bc4a4c9845e37cf32233de29bf0ae626759d667932655588b3f07b`
**pt-BR review revision:** `ccbefffb6887b3f4bb1d5619f191962bf1c1496746bd2231a3b71ef7c79594fd`

### English

Why is Git classified as distributed version control?

1. Branches exist only on the hosting service

   Git branches can exist entirely locally.

2. Clones can hold project history locally **[CORRECT]**

   Local repositories can contain history and support independent work.

3. Every edit requires the central server

   Git permits many operations without a network connection.

4. All contributors share one working directory

   Each contributor can use an independent working directory.

**Concept:** Distributed repositories support local history and offline version-control operations.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Por qué Git se clasifica como control de versiones distribuido?

1. Las ramas solo existen en el servicio de alojamiento

   Las ramas de Git pueden existir solo localmente.

2. Los clones pueden conservar el historial localmente **[CORRECT]**

   Los repositorios locales pueden contener historial y permitir trabajo independiente.

3. Cada edición requiere el servidor central

   Git permite muchas operaciones sin conexión de red.

4. Todos comparten un directorio de trabajo

   Cada colaborador puede usar un directorio de trabajo independiente.

**Concept:** Los repositorios distribuidos permiten historial local y operaciones sin conexión.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Por que Git é classificado como controle de versão distribuído?

1. Branches existem apenas no serviço de hospedagem

   Branches do Git podem existir apenas localmente.

2. Clones podem manter o histórico localmente **[CORRECT]**

   Repositórios locais podem conter histórico e permitir trabalho independente.

3. Cada edição exige o servidor central

   Git permite muitas operações sem conexão de rede.

4. Todos compartilham um diretório de trabalho

   Cada colaborador pode usar um diretório de trabalho independente.

**Concept:** Repositórios distribuídos permitem histórico local e operações sem conexão.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

**en review revision:** `e8cb71bc854a4e871dd4adebf3ef8d67a5010f248d2cd8a2c3ac8e88ee8c6dc9`
**es review revision:** `34b3e16f26170f81029970bb389fd1554e9c7a0faf8bea6c45bd05e12f204db9`
**pt-BR review revision:** `dfc8035fada7b2ae4c38440b792f18f8e9d888fdc123cc1d437c3b935b1b2c74`

### English

Which command identifies staged, unstaged, and untracked files?

1. git branch

   Branch lists or manages branch references.

2. git remote

   Remote manages repository connection aliases.

3. git status **[CORRECT]**

   Status reports the working-tree and staging-area state.

4. git log

   Log displays commits rather than current file status.

**Concept:** Status distinguishes current file changes from recorded commit history.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué comando identifica archivos preparados, modificados sin preparar y sin seguimiento?

1. git branch

   Branch enumera o gestiona referencias de ramas.

2. git remote

   Remote gestiona alias de conexión a repositorios.

3. git status **[CORRECT]**

   Status informa del directorio de trabajo y del área de preparación.

4. git log

   Log muestra commits, no el estado actual de los archivos.

**Concept:** Status distingue los cambios actuales del historial de commits registrado.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Qual comando identifica arquivos preparados, modificados sem staging e não rastreados?

1. git branch

   Branch lista ou gerencia referências de branches.

2. git remote

   Remote gerencia aliases de conexão com repositórios.

3. git status **[CORRECT]**

   Status informa o estado da árvore de trabalho e da área de staging.

4. git log

   Log mostra commits, não o estado atual dos arquivos.

**Concept:** Status distingue alterações atuais do histórico de commits registrado.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-020`

**Content revision:** `212dd18e61b35c729a5410757b84e0669e5ffe2bedc44250f96d718e79ea2f8b`

**en review revision:** `6a3b308bf9f07f5976587988c0b5174f7c6abcc9e5e087407a6890db590e1d97`
**es review revision:** `774f33d86d9db80a36b89db4ab7d326e4cacfa87be4cb262d83b5560d4a88db3`
**pt-BR review revision:** `ab0ccef545f1077b81d19515e9601bd8b76d2a41d9b79b285e3a0dc2005487be`

### English

For tracked files, what does plain git diff show by default?

1. All staged changes relative to HEAD

   The staged comparison requires --staged or --cached.

2. All commits not yet pushed to origin

   Unpushed commits require comparing commit histories.

3. All differences between remote branches

   Plain diff does not automatically compare remote branches.

4. Unstaged changes relative to the index **[CORRECT]**

   Plain diff compares tracked working-tree content with the index.

**Concept:** The default diff compares unstaged edits with staged content.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

Para archivos con seguimiento, ¿qué muestra git diff sin opciones por defecto?

1. Todos los cambios preparados respecto a HEAD

   Comparar lo preparado requiere --staged o --cached.

2. Todos los commits aún no subidos a origin

   Los commits sin subir requieren comparar historiales.

3. Todas las diferencias entre ramas remotas

   Diff sin opciones no compara automáticamente ramas remotas.

4. Cambios sin preparar respecto al índice **[CORRECT]**

   Diff compara el contenido del directorio de trabajo con el índice.

**Concept:** La comparación predeterminada de diff enfrenta ediciones sin preparar y contenido preparado.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Para arquivos rastreados, o que git diff sem opções mostra por padrão?

1. Todas as alterações preparadas em relação a HEAD

   Comparar o staging exige --staged ou --cached.

2. Todos os commits ainda não enviados ao origin

   Commits não enviados exigem comparar históricos.

3. Todas as diferenças entre branches remotas

   Diff sem opções não compara branches remotas automaticamente.

4. Alterações sem staging em relação ao índice **[CORRECT]**

   Diff compara o conteúdo da árvore de trabalho com o índice.

**Concept:** A comparação padrão de diff confronta edições sem staging e conteúdo preparado.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

### English

Which command initializes Git metadata in the current folder?

1. git init **[CORRECT]**

   Init creates repository metadata in the current directory.

2. git clone

   Clone copies an existing repository into a local repository.

3. git add

   Add requires a repository and stages content.

4. git commit

   Commit records staged content in an existing repository.

**Concept:** Initialization creates a repository; staging and committing are separate operations.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

2. git clone

   Clone copia un repositorio existente a uno local.

3. git add

   Add requiere un repositorio y prepara contenido.

4. git commit

   Commit registra contenido preparado en un repositorio existente.

**Concept:** Inicializar crea un repositorio; preparar y confirmar son operaciones distintas.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

   Init cria metadados do repositório no diretório atual.

   Clone copia um repositório existente para um local.

3. git add

   Add exige um repositório e prepara conteúdo.

4. git commit

   Commit registra conteúdo preparado em um repositório existente.

## Question 22: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-022`

**Content revision:** `3007b4e9b458e2d844b406904ed27122f4fbade505e4068f35986fabaa8a3ecf`

**en review revision:** `9f56de9839008aedcdf8abd86099d86935e7285d50a8739783745f16bc266d73`
**es review revision:** `0944e30ab4d1dab14773b88584987af37777f6d91398721a17648099ed50cbd7`
**pt-BR review revision:** `1c7b5cb0697c70e7c277b86e4f274c1d6b8006f2f06272cc4f42c32736991f05`

### English

In a non-bare Git repository, what is the working tree?

1. The metadata identifying a signed release

   Release-signing metadata does not define the working tree.

2. The checked-out files available for editing **[CORRECT]**

   The working tree contains the files currently checked out.

3. The content selected for the next commit

   Selected content is stored in the index or staging area.

4. The complete collection of remote branches

   Remote branches are references, not the working files.

**Concept:** The working tree, index, and commit history are distinct Git concepts.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

En un repositorio Git no bare, ¿qué es el directorio de trabajo?

1. Los metadatos de una versión firmada

   Los metadatos de firma no definen el directorio de trabajo.

2. Los archivos extraídos disponibles para editar **[CORRECT]**

   El directorio de trabajo contiene los archivos actualmente extraídos.

3. El contenido seleccionado para el próximo commit

   El contenido seleccionado se almacena en el índice.

4. El conjunto completo de ramas remotas

   Las ramas remotas son referencias, no archivos de trabajo.

**Concept:** El directorio de trabajo, el índice y el historial de commits son conceptos distintos.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Em um repositório Git não bare, o que é a árvore de trabalho?

1. Os metadados de uma versão assinada

   Metadados de assinatura não definem a árvore de trabalho.

2. Os arquivos em checkout disponíveis para edição **[CORRECT]**

   A árvore de trabalho contém os arquivos atualmente em checkout.

3. O conteúdo selecionado para o próximo commit

   O conteúdo selecionado fica no índice ou staging.

4. O conjunto completo de branches remotas

   Branches remotas são referências, não arquivos de trabalho.

**Concept:** Árvore de trabalho, índice e histórico de commits são conceitos distintos.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie os arquivos em edição do histórico salvo do projeto.
- Considere se a operação registra, inspeciona ou combina trabalho.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

### English

Which Git reference is commonly used to mark a release commit?

1. A stash

   A stash temporarily saves working changes.

2. An index

   The index selects content for the next commit.

3. A tag **[CORRECT]**

   Tags name particular points in repository history.

4. A remote

   A remote names a connection to another repository.

**Concept:** A tag provides a recognizable name for a selected commit.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Separate the files you are editing from saved project history.
- Consider whether the operation records, inspects, or combines work.

<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

1. Un stash

   Un stash guarda temporalmente cambios de trabajo.

2. Un índice

   El índice selecciona contenido para el próximo commit.

3. Una etiqueta **[CORRECT]**

   Las etiquetas nombran puntos concretos del historial.

4. Un remoto

   Un remoto nombra una conexión con otro repositorio.

**Concept:** Una etiqueta proporciona un nombre reconocible a un commit seleccionado.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue los archivos que editas del historial guardado del proyecto.
- Considera si la operación registra, inspecciona o combina trabajo.

</details>

<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

2. Um índice

   O índice seleciona conteúdo para o próximo commit.

3. Uma tag **[CORRECT]**

   Tags nomeiam pontos específicos do histórico.

4. Um remoto

   Um remoto nomeia uma conexão com outro repositório.

**Concept:** Uma tag fornece um nome reconhecível a um commit selecionado.

**Authored coaching hints:**

**Concept:** Markdown editing reserves some shortcuts; the palette has an alternate default shortcut.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-025`

**Content revision:** `97e736d54b2bc30e70224061578ede3411e11bbc318f20ad436f7b281b45d1dd`

**en review revision:** `a7b25a2fbc370becde192ffb428857d0cb394863117f54a6fb0572e7c03341d8`
**es review revision:** `affc079117ef7af58b35ff78d7dcf7f64a4bc96a9de46f5b0633d5e7592c809a`
**pt-BR review revision:** `00df8be78ea64b268d12456f5c239a304a066484437adb7168aa1459e32f43fb`

### English

For a newly opened pull request, which branch is normally selected as the base by default?

1. The repository's default branch **[CORRECT]**

   The repository default branch is the normal initial base.

2. The author's oldest feature branch

   The author's branch age does not determine the base.

3. The latest release's tag

   A tag identifies a commit, not a pull request base branch.

4. The most recently deleted branch

   Deleted branches are not selected as the default base.

**Concept:** The base is the branch that would receive proposed changes.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Think about the stages of a branch-based contribution.
- Separate proposing a change from making it part of another branch.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

Al abrir un pull request nuevo, ¿qué rama se selecciona normalmente como base por defecto?

1. La rama predeterminada del repositorio **[CORRECT]**

   La rama predeterminada suele ser la base inicial.

2. La rama de trabajo más antigua del autor

   La antigüedad de las ramas del autor no determina la base.

3. La etiqueta de la última versión

   Una etiqueta identifica un commit, no una rama base.

4. La rama eliminada más recientemente

   Las ramas eliminadas no se seleccionan como base predeterminada.

**Concept:** La base es la rama que recibiría los cambios propuestos.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Piensa en las etapas de una contribución basada en ramas.
- Distingue proponer un cambio de incorporarlo a otra rama.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

Ao abrir um novo pull request, qual branch normalmente é selecionada como base por padrão?

1. A branch padrão do repositório **[CORRECT]**

   A branch padrão costuma ser a base inicial.

2. A branch de trabalho mais antiga do autor

   A idade das branches do autor não determina a base.

3. A tag da versão mais recente

   Uma tag identifica um commit, não uma branch base.

4. A branch excluída mais recentemente

   Branches excluídas não são selecionadas como base padrão.

**Concept:** A base é a branch que receberia as alterações propostas.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Pense nas etapas de uma contribuição baseada em branches.
- Diferencie propor uma alteração de incorporá-la a outra branch.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/repositories/configuring-branches-and-merges-in-your-repository/managing-branches-in-your-repository/changing-the-default-branch>

## Question 26: Understand Git and GitHub basics

- [ ] English content approved: I verified the question, choices, correct answer, explanations, concept, hints, and supporting documentation.

- [ ] Spanish translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

- [ ] Brazilian Portuguese translation approved: I verified faithful translation of the approved English question, choices, answer explanations, concept, hints, and documentation references.

**Question ID:** `ghf-026`

**Content revision:** `48235943fc3c2813d3f5215f1b64f69270bd5375ee39d250b220c5c7eccc264e`

**en review revision:** `55db873cb90bb9cf68ad82744ec890742d1c4fe57bec16463ae867e8f66731f5`
**es review revision:** `ef40d4993f1dceea9139bbbbd46bca26c0ba74cd723b592cdf61f6a27c66c3c9`
**pt-BR review revision:** `a6c52ccd0c141c755723cc14d973b689cde783f53b2d84d3e5fd3aa650cdea2a`

### English

Which statement distinguishes a GitHub fork from a Git clone?

1. A fork copies only tags; a clone copies only branch names

   Both operations involve repository content, not only reference names.

2. A fork is a related hosted repository; a clone is a local copy **[CORRECT]**

   Forking creates a related repository; cloning brings Git data locally.

3. A fork is a local copy; a clone is a related hosted repository

   This reverses forking and cloning.

4. A fork copies only issues; a clone copies only pull requests

   Neither operation is defined as copying only collaboration items.

**Concept:** Forking and cloning serve different repository ownership and location needs.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<details>
<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Español

¿Qué diferencia un fork de GitHub de un clon de Git?

1. Un fork copia solo etiquetas; un clon copia solo nombres de ramas

   Ambas incluyen contenido del repositorio, no solo nombres de referencias.

2. Un fork es un repositorio alojado relacionado; un clon es una copia local **[CORRECT]**

   El fork crea un repositorio relacionado; el clon lleva los datos Git al equipo.

3. Un fork es una copia local; un clon es un repositorio alojado relacionado

   Esta opción invierte fork y clon.

4. Un fork copia solo issues; un clon copia solo pull requests

   Ninguna operación se define como copiar solo elementos colaborativos.

**Concept:** Fork y clon responden a necesidades distintas de propiedad y ubicación del repositorio.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distingue el historial local del historial de otro repositorio.
- Comprueba si se necesita descargar, integrar o subir cambios.

</details>

<details>
<summary>Português (Brasil) — same answer key and option order</summary>

### Português (Brasil)

O que diferencia um fork do GitHub de um clone do Git?

1. Um fork copia apenas tags; um clone copia apenas nomes de branches

   Ambas envolvem conteúdo do repositório, não apenas nomes de referências.

2. Um fork é um repositório hospedado relacionado; um clone é uma cópia local **[CORRECT]**

   O fork cria um repositório relacionado; o clone traz os dados Git para o computador.

3. Um fork é uma cópia local; um clone é um repositório hospedado relacionado

   Esta opção inverte fork e clone.

4. Um fork copia apenas issues; um clone copia apenas pull requests

   Nenhuma operação se define como copiar apenas itens colaborativos.

**Concept:** Fork e clone atendem a necessidades diferentes de propriedade e localização do repositório.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Diferencie o histórico local do histórico de outro repositório.
- Verifique se é necessário baixar, integrar ou enviar alterações.

</details>

**Official supporting source:** <https://docs.github.com/en/get-started/using-git/about-git>

Which operation can succeed offline in an existing local Git repository?

   Sending commits to GitHub requires connectivity.

2. Open a pull request on GitHub

3. Commit already staged changes **[CORRECT]**

   Committing staged changes records history locally.

4. Fetch new commits from GitHub

   Fetching new GitHub data requires connectivity.

**Concept:** Local version-control work does not always require the hosting service.

**Authored coaching hints:**

- Distinguish local history from history on another repository.
- Check whether downloading, integrating, or uploading is required.

<summary>Español — same answer key and option order</summary>

¿Qué operación puede completarse sin conexión en un repositorio Git local existente?

2. Abrir un pull request en GitHub

   Crear un pull request alojado requiere acceder a GitHub.

3. Confirmar cambios ya preparados **[CORRECT]**

   Confirmar cambios preparados registra el historial localmente.

4. Obtener commits nuevos de GitHub

   Obtener datos nuevos de GitHub requiere conectividad.

**Concept:** El control de versiones local no siempre requiere el servicio de alojamiento.

**Authored coaching hints:**

</details>

   Enviar commits para o GitHub exige conectividade.

2. Abrir um pull request no GitHub

   Criar um pull request hospedado exige acessar o GitHub.

3. Confirmar alterações já preparadas **[CORRECT]**

   Confirmar alterações preparadas registra o histórico localmente.

4. Obter novos commits do GitHub

   Obter novos dados do GitHub exige conectividade.

**Concept:** O controle de versão local nem sempre exige o serviço de hospedagem.

**Authored coaching hints:**
